\texttt{Trial \#2: New plan: I failed because I executed the recipe steps in the wrong order and used incorrect ingredients. My new strategy is: 1) Read the potion recipe in the bedroom immediately to confirm the exact steps. 2) Clear the cauldron of any incorrect items (toad, eye of newt) if possible, or start fresh if the game allows. 3) Collect ingredients in the strict order: Ectoplasm (broom closet), Spider (cellar), Banana (pantry). 4) Perform the wand actions (Fairy then Ice) from the magic workshop. 5) Finally, add the Green beetle (cellar). I will not add any item to the cauldron until I have verified it is the correct next step in the sequence.} \\
